Surprisingly, we find that trai","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-01T17:59:54Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2607_01232.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作问的是一个很具体、但此前几乎没人系统回答的问题：RL post-training 的收益到底分布在 Transformer 的哪些层里，是否真的需要全参数一起更新。作者不是从参数高效训练的角度出发，而是把它当成一个结构性问题来研究：在固定预训练模型的前提下，若只训练单层或少数层，能恢复多少 full-parameter RL 的收益；这种层间差异是否稳定，是否跨算法、跨数据、跨任务一致。全文给出的答案很明确：RL 适配并不均匀，收益高度集中在中间层，而且这种集中性有相当强的稳定结构。","importance":"对基座研究的意义主要有两层。第一，它给 RL post-training 提供了一个比“全模型一起调”更细的结构视角。预训练模型在深度方向上本来就不均匀，这篇工作说明 RL 也继承了这种非均匀性，而且集中得比很多人预期更强。第二，它不只是解释现象，还把这个现象转成了可操作的训练策略：优先训练高贡献层，既能减少更新参数量，也可能超过 full-parameter RL。对 pretrain↔posttrain 接口的启发在于，RL 并不一定是在全网重写能力，更像是在少数关键层上重排已有表征与决策边界。这会影响后续对 mid-training、layer-wise LR、adapter 放置位置、甚至 continual RL 的设计。","technical_approach":"方法上并不复杂，但实验设计很干净。作者定义了 layer contribution：对第 l 层单独做 RL 训练、其余层冻结，然后看它相对 base model 的提升，能恢复 full-parameter RL 提升的多少比例。这个量本质上是一个归一化恢复率，便于跨模型和任务比较。围绕这个指标，全文做了三类实验。\n\n第一类是单层隔离实验。对每一层分别单独做 RL，比较不同层的贡献。结果显示最优单层可恢复 full RL 收益的 114%，最差层不到 30%。这说明“单层是否足够”不是一个统一答案，而是强依赖层位。\n\n第二类是稳定性分析。作者在 Qwen3 与 Qwen2.5 两个家族、7 个模型、3 种 RL 算法（GRPO、GiGPO、Dr. GRPO）、3 类任务（数学、代码、agentic decision-making）上重复测量 layer contribution。结论是高贡献层稳定集中在中部；同一模型在不同数学数据集 NuminaMath-CoT 与 DeepScaleR 上的层排序相关性达到 Spearman ρ=0.76，在数学与代码任务间也有 ρ=0.59。这说明它不是某个数据集偶然现象，而更像预训练模型内部已有的层功能分工被 RL 选择性利用。\n\n第三类是算法利用。作者据此提出 layer-aware RL：不再默认全层同权更新，而是优先训练高贡献层。文中给出的代表性结果是 Qwen3-8B 上只训练贡献最高的 10 层，数学 benchmark 平均准确率 69.1%，高于 full-parameter RL 的 66.4%。此外，不同层训练出的模型在解题行为上有互补性，做 majority voting 还能继续提升。这一点很有意思，说明不同层的 RL 适配可能对应不同的策略偏置，而不是简单的强弱关系。\n\n从机制上看，作者的隐含判断是：中间层更像“可塑的推理变换层”，底层更偏词法/局部表征，顶层更贴近输出分布与语言表面形式，因此 RL 的可验证奖励更容易在中间层形成高杠杆更新。论文没有把这个机制完全证明，但证据链是连贯的。与常见 LoRA/PEFT 工作不同，这里不是先假设低秩或少参数足够，而是先测量哪里最有用，再据此设计训练。","experiments":"关键实验覆盖面比较完整。模型方面包括 Qwen3、Qwen2.5 两个系列，共 7 个模型；算法方面包括 GRPO、GiGPO、Dr. GRPO；任务覆盖数学推理、代码生成和 agentic decision-making。最核心的数字有三个：\n\n1. 单层上限：最佳单层可恢复 full-parameter RL 提升的 114%，最弱层低于 30%。这说明层间差异不是小幅波动，而是数量级上可见的结构差异。\n2. 排名稳定性：同一模型在 NuminaMath-CoT 与 DeepScaleR 之间的 per-layer contribution 排名 Spearman ρ=0.76；NuminaMath-CoT 与 DeepCoder 之间 ρ=0.59。跨数据、跨任务仍保持中等到较强相关。\n3. 层选择训练收益：在 Qwen3-8B 上，仅训练 top-10 高贡献层，数学 benchmark 平均准确率 69.1%，超过 full-parameter RL 的 66.4%。\n\n从消融角度，最值得注意的是“结构性而非随机性”这一点。若层贡献只是噪声，那么不同任务和算法下排序应高度不稳定；但文中结果相反。另一个重要观察是，不同层训练出的模型存在互补错误模式，投票优于单模型。这提示 layer-wise RL 可能在探索不同策略子空间，而 full-parameter RL 反而会把这种多样性压平。\n\n如果从预训练研究者角度看，还应留意作者是否报告了训练预算等价性。文中 layer contribution 是在 iso-setting 下比较恢复率，这一点很关键，因为否则单层训练可能只是因为更容易优化、而不是更有表达杠杆。论文的主张建立在这种受控比较上。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读到中度精读，重点不是公式，而是实验设计和图表。建议先看 Introduction 把问题设定吃透，再直接看定义 layer contribution 的方法部分，确认比较口径。随后重点看三组图：\n\n1. 每层 contribution 曲线。这里能直接看出中间层峰值是否尖锐、不同模型是否同形。\n2. 不同数据集/任务之间的层排序相关图。这里决定“结构稳定”这个结论是否站得住。\n3. layer-aware 训练与 full-parameter RL 的对比表，以及 ensemble/majority voting 结果。这里体现论文从分析走向方法的价值。\n\n阅读时建议带着两个问题：第一，为什么中间层最适合承接 RL 奖励信号，而不是顶层；第二，这个现象是 RL 特有，还是任何强监督 post-training 都会出现。若后者成立，论文价值更多在统一解释；若前者成立，则说明 RL 奖励与深层表征之间有更特殊的耦合。预计 30-45 分钟可以抓住主要结论；若要深挖，可继续看 appendix 里的不同模型和算法复现实验。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a precise structural question about RL post-training: where do the gains actually come from inside a pretrained transformer, and do we really need to update the whole network to obtain them? Instead of treating this as a parameter-efficiency trick, the authors study it as a layer-wise adaptation problem. They measure how much of the full-parameter RL improvement can be recovered when only one layer, or a selected subset of layers, is trained while the rest are frozen. The central finding is clear: RL adaptation is highly non-uniform across depth, with most of the useful change concentrated in middle layers.","importance":"Its value for foundation-model research is twofold. First, it gives a sharper structural view of post-training. Pretrained transformers are already known to be depth-wise heterogeneous; this work shows that RL inherits and amplifies that heterogeneity rather than distributing gains evenly across layers. Second, the paper turns that observation into a practical training recipe. If a small set of high-contribution layers can match or exceed full-parameter RL, then RL may be acting less like a global rewrite and more like a targeted reconfiguration of a few leverage points in the pretrained network. That matters for layer-wise optimization, adapter placement, continual RL, and the broader pretrain-to-posttrain interface.","technical_approach":"$26","experiments":"$27","reading_guide":"$28","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"反常识发现：RL 后训练的收益高度集中在单层更新中，对后训练效率优化和预训练层设计有直接启发，重点读 §4 实验与层贡献分析。","en":"Counterintuitive finding: RL post-training gains are highly concentrated in single-layer updates; directly informs post-training efficiency and pre-training layer design, focus on §4 experiments and layer contribution analysis."},"metadata":{"institutions":["University of Minnesota","Peking University"],"authors":["Zijian Zhang","Rizhen Hu","Athanasios Glentis","Dawei Li","Chung-Yiu Yau","Hongzhou Lin","Mingyi Hong"],"notable_authors":["Mingyi Hong"],"tags":["RL post-training","transformer layers","layer contribution","mechanism analysis","parameter-efficient fine-tuning"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01232","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.01232","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2607.01033","title":"The Model Organism Lottery: Model Organism Interpretability Strongly Depends on Training Methodology","abstract":"Model organisms (MOs) - language models trained to exhibit undesired or unnatural behaviours - are frequently used as testbeds for evaluating white-box interpretability techniques. Current MOs are typically constructed via post-hoc supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on behavioural transcripts or synthetic documents. Prior research has shown that interpretability methods can easily identify hidden behaviours in these MOs. However, recent work suggests that such post-hoc training methods may make interp","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-01T15:01:30Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2607_01033.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"当前用于白盒可解释性技术评测的主流测试集是模型有机体（Model Organism, MO）——即被刻意训练出特定目标行为的语言模型。现有MO几乎都通过窄域后处理监督微调构造，这类MO上测得的可解释性结果能否外推到常规训练中自然涌现的行为，以及MO构造方法对可解释性评测结果的影响程度，此前没有系统研究。","importance":"结果说明基于窄域SFT构造MO的基准得到的可解释性结果普遍偏乐观，无法可靠代表方法在真实模型上的表现。更重要的是，工作提供了明确证据：相同的外在行为可以由完全不同的内部表征支撑，训练方式（后处理SFT/DPO/融入正式后训练流程）对表征结构的影响远大于行为层面的相似性，这对理解post-training如何修改模型内部表征、设计更可靠的对齐评测方法都有参考。","technical_approach":"构造了严格控制变量的MO测试集，共54个模型，基于OLMo2-1B和Gemma-3-1b-it两个底座，覆盖三类良性触发-反应行为：烘焙错误事实、食物语境下的意大利餐偏好、军事语境下的潜艇话题执念。每类行为下分别用7种训练方式构造MO变体：标准后处理转录蒸馏（TD）、合成文档微调（SDF），两种方法分别设置加/不加无关通用数据混合的版本；后处理DPO，同样设置加/不加通用数据混合的版本；以及更贴近真实流程的集成式训练：将行为相关数据直接混入OLMo原生的DPO后训练阶段共同训练。所有MO都校准到相同的行为触发率和反应强度，且控制行为不会泄露到无关语境。之后用四类主流白盒可解释性方法（激活预言机、基于激活差的引导、logit lens方法、稀疏自编码器）测试每个MO中植入行为的可检测程度，控制变量对比训练方法、行为类型、模型架构的影响。","experiments":"核心结果包括三点：第一，外在行为完全匹配的MO，仅因训练方式不同，可解释性方法的检测成功率差异可达1.2-20.4倍；第二，传统窄域后处理SFT构造的MO可检测性最高，即最容易被解释，而最贴近真实后训练流程的集成式DPO构造的MO可检测性显著最低；第三，不同可解释性方法在不同训练方式MO上的表现排序，在不同行为类型、不同模型架构之间没有一致的泛化性，即一个方法在某类MO上表现好，不代表在其他训练方式得到的MO上同样有效。结果对训练随机性、数据顺序鲁棒，不是随机波动导致。全部54个MO、训练数据和代码已开源。","reading_guide":"泛读重点关注核心结论部分——相同外在行为在不同训练方式下的内部表征可解释性差异，以及对现有MO基准有效性的讨论，这部分对理解post-training对表征的影响最有价值。各类MO的具体训练超参数、可解释性方法的实现细节可以跳过，如果做可解释性相关工作，再参考3.3节的集成式MO构造方法和3.4节的行为匹配控制流程。预计阅读时间15分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Model organisms (MOs) — language models intentionally trained to exhibit specific target behaviors — are the standard testbed for white-box interpretability evaluation. Nearly all existing MOs are built via narrow post-hoc supervised fine-tuning, and it remains untested whether interpretability results on these artificial MOs generalize to behaviors that emerge during regular model training, or how strongly MO construction methodology affects measured interpretability performance.","importance":"Results show that interpretability benchmarks built on narrow SFT MOs produce consistently overoptimistic results, and do not reliably predict method performance on models trained via more realistic pipelines. Beyond implications for interpretability evaluation, the work provides clear evidence that identical observable behavior can be supported by drastically different internal representations: training methodology (post-hoc SFT / DPO / integrated post-training) has a far larger effect on representational structure than surface-level behavioral similarity. This is relevant to understanding how post-training modifies model internals, and to designing more robust alignment evaluation protocols.","technical_approach":"A controlled suite of 54 MOs is constructed on OLMo2-1B and gemma-3-1b-it bases, covering three benign trigger-reaction quirks: false cake baking facts, Italian food preference in food contexts, and submarine fixation in military contexts. For each quirk, 7 training variants are produced: standard post-hoc transcript distillation and synthetic document fine-tuning (each with and without unrelated general data mixing), post-hoc DPO (with and without general data mixing), and a novel integrated construction method that inserts quirk data directly into OLMo’s official DPO post-training pipeline. All MOs are calibrated to identical quirk trigger rates and response strength, with controls to prevent quirk leakage into irrelevant contexts. Four standard white-box interpretability techniques (activation oracles, activation difference steering, logit lens, sparse autoencoders) are used to measure quirk detectability across all MOs under controlled conditions.","experiments":"Three core findings emerge: First, MOs with identical observable behavior exhibit 1.2–20.4× variation in quirk detectability, driven solely by training method. Second, standard narrow post-hoc SFT MOs are the most easily interpretable, while integrated DPO MOs (closest to realistic post-training pipelines) are consistently the least detectable. Third, performance rankings of interpretability methods across training protocols do not generalize across quirk types or model architectures, meaning strong performance on one MO variant does not predict performance on others. Results are robust to training nondeterminism and data ordering, and are not explained by random variation. All 54 MOs, training data, and code are open-sourced.","reading_guide":"Focus on the core result that behaviorally identical models have drastically different interpretability based on training method, and the discussion of limitations in current MO benchmark design — these are the most relevant insights for understanding post-training representational changes. atlas，包括具体功能、能力类别和更高层级的认知结构。框架还分析 parcel 之间的结构连接，并把这些内部单元与输出行为、异常行为、人类皮层反应和经典认知模型联系起来。与局部可解释性工作相比，它关注的是中观层级：比单个 feature 更稳定，比整体 embedding 更可解释。","experiments":"论文报告了几类实验。第一，parcels alignment。若关注预训练机制，重点不是脑科学类比本身，而是这套中观表征分析是否能用于比较不同 checkpoint、不同数据配比、不同 post-training 前后的内部功能重排。对实验细节要保持谨慎，尤其要检查自动标注、任务集合选择和 parcel 稳定性是否会影响结论。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether LLMs have a stable and interpretable internal cognitive organization, and whether that organization can explain capabilities, failures, and links to human cortical activity. It moves beyond isolated neurons or individual circuits and tries to build a system-level map from sparse internal features to functional parcels, cognitive capabilities, and hierarchical structure.","importance":"The contribution is most relevant to interpretability and model diagnostics rather than to pretraining recipes directly. NeuroCogMap offers a way to study how capabilities are distributed inside a trained model, which functional units are conserved across models, and how different failure modes affect different internal subsystems. For pretraining research, the useful angle is comparative analysis: the same framework could be applied across checkpoints, architectures, data mixtures, or post-training stages to see how internal organization changes.","technical_approach":"NeuroCogMap starts from a broad capability space and associated evaluation datasets. It collects internal activations from LLMs and extracts sparse model features, in the spirit of decomposing high-dimensional representations into more interpretable components. These features are then grouped into functional parcels based on coherence and functional association. Each parcel is linked to a cognitive atlas of functions, then aggregated into higher-level capabilities and a cognitive hierarchy. The framework also studies interactions between parcels and relates parcel activity to outputs, pathological behaviors, human cortical responses, and classical cognitive models. Compared with local mechanistic interpretability, the method targets a middle scale: more stable than individual features, but more interpretable than whole-layer representations.","experiments":"The paper reports that the derived parcels are stable, semantically coherent, and partly conserved across models. Pathology analyses separate representational failures from behavioral-control failures, showing different disruption patterns across circuits, parcels, capabilities, and hierarchy. These signatures are then used for detection and mechanism-guided mitigation. In human-alignment experiments, parcel activity improves cortical-response prediction during naturalistic language comprehension compared with conventional model representations, and the most aligned parcels correspond to higher-order cortical systems in both functional profile and semantic description. The paper also uses latent strategy signatures from LLM behavioral simulations to refine classical decision-making models and improve held-out behavioral fit. The provided content does not include exact model sizes, data scales, sparse-feature settings, or numerical gains, so those should be checked in the tables and ablations.","reading_guide":"A 30–45 minute skim is appropriate. Focus first on the framework construction: sparse feature extraction, parcel formation, cognitive-atlas annotation, and hierarchy building. Then read the results on cross-model stability, pathology signatures, and cortical alignment. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-01T16:16:07Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2607_01131.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是“让 系统大多停在单步假设生成，缺少对“已知什么、还缺什么、哪些变量被反复涉及、是否存在共同混杂因素”的显式反思机制，因此难以形成真正开放式、累积式的发现过程。DiscoPER 试图把这个缺口补上。","importance":"对基座预训练本身，它不是直接改训练目标或优化器的论文，因此重要性更多体现在“LLM 作为研究代理时，什么样的外部执行与记忆结构能把语言能力变成可靠探索能力”。有三点启发比较实在。第一，它把 discovery loop 明确拆成 Propose-Evaluate-Reflect，并把 reflection 从提示工程层面的总结，提升为对 claim store 的结构化分析，这对后续研究 agent、长期记忆、工具使用和 test-time search 都有参考价值。第二，它坚持每个发现都要落到可执行代码和统计检验，而不是让模型直接口头下结论，这说明在高风险开放探索任务里，语言模型更适合做“假设生成器+程序接口”，而不是证据本身。第三，它构造了更接近开放式发现的 benchmark，包括来自 iNaturalist 的 iNatDisco，这类评测资产对研究“模型是否真的在探索，而不是在复述常见模式”有一定价值。若从预训练研究员视角看，这篇更像 agentic scientific discovery 的方法论文，而不是 pretrain 核心论文。","technical_approach":"方法上最关键的是把开放式科学发现形式化为一个带持久状态的循环系统。数据集记为 X，系统在每轮根据当前已接受 claim 集合 C_t、已拒绝假设集合和 guidance G_t 生成 K 个新假设。这里的假设不是自然语言句子，而是可执行程序：程序读取数据，运行统计检验，输出 supported/rejected 以及证据。只有同时满足显著性和效应量阈值的结果才会进入 claim store。这样做的目的很明确：把 LLM 的自由表达能力限制在“提出候选分析脚本”，而把真假判断交给外部统计程序。\n\nDiscoPER 的核心增量在 Reflect。作者不是简单把历史发现拼接回上下文，而是让系统周期性分析 claim store，识别两类信息：一类是 under-explored variables，即哪些变量很少被研究；另一类是 recurring confounds，即多个已发现关系里反复出现的潜在共同因素。基于这些模式，系统生成下一轮 guidance，指导后续 hypothesis proposal。这个设计对应科学研究里常见的两种推进方式：补空白，以及从多个局部相关中抽象出更高阶结构。\n\n从论文描述看，它还是一个多代理、代码执行、统计验证、持久记忆结合的框架。与传统结构学习方法相比，它的假设空间更自由，不局限于成对关系或固定图结构；与需要给定研究问题的 AI scientist 系统相比，它不依赖 seed question；与普通 code agent 相比，它多了显式的 claim accumulation 和 meta-reflection。作者还强调使用 held-out split 验证发现，目的是减少在同一数据上反复试探带来的伪发现风险。这个点虽然不能完全解决多重比较问题，但至少比“在训练集上不断挖模式再直接汇报”更严谨。","experiments":"论文给出的主结果是：DiscoPER 在多个 discovery benchmark 上优于对比方法，并且 reflection 模块能提升探索质量；在新构建的 iNatDisco 上，系统可以重新发现超过 60% 的人类专家验证模式。这个数字是全文里最值得记住的结果，因为它直接对应“开放式发现是否真的找到了现实世界中已知但未显式提供给模型的规律”。\n\n从引言和方法部分能确认的实验设计重点有几项。第一，benchmark 不再是给定问题后做验证，而是从真实科学数据中构造“可开放发现”的任务，并提供 literature-grounded 的 expert-verified claims 作为近似真值。第二，系统输出必须经过统计显著性和效应量筛选，说明评估不只看语言表述是否像发现，而看是否有数据支持。第三，作者专门比较了有无 reflection 的版本，用来验证 claim-level memory
